**Three counts in the first record of this close were wrong and are corrected here.** It said *twelve* repairs,
which matched no measure above; it said eleven chapters were reopened, which omitted **292, 297 and 298**; and it
said those chapters fell across *three* batches, when they fall across **four**. The reopened list is the
nineteen in the table. **Four duplicated paragraphs across three chapters** is the correct figure for the
duplication repairs — 248 ×1, 275 ×2, 295 ×1 — and the earlier *five paragraphs* was an overcount.

### Eight findings recorded and not repaired, with the reason

**Finding 1 was recorded and not repaired at the close and has been ruled on and repaired at the close review.
It is kept here, in place, because the ruling is the record.** The close had decided it was an authorised motif
and left it standing; the review held that the close's own position on authorial scaffolding could not be held
at the same time, and asked for an explicit ruling instead of a deferral to Batch 0002's original audit.

**RULING — the Yuan tag is not authorial scaffolding, and the two positions can both stand.** The close cut
seven instances of scaffolding — *in this book*, *for the rest of the book*, *in five volumes*, *four volumes* —
because they are talk about the **novel's own construction**: its length, its volumes, its reader. The tag names
neither. It names a **diegetic register rule** — his speech is in the present tense, which is the standing
consequence of his frame — and a **dramatic fact about the scene** — the line bought nothing. It is a ledger
line, not a remark about how the book is built, and it survives the test the scaffolding instances fail.
**So the device was not deleted and Batch 0002's motif was not struck.**

**But the run itself was a fault, and it is repaired.** Eleven near-identical sentences across eleven of fifteen
consecutive chapters, with **five byte-identical** and four more byte-identical in a second variant, is squarely
inside `AGENTS.md`'s condition on the same construction used more than twice in a row — and the close had already
applied exactly that standard to *about four seconds*, thinning four uses to two, which is why the two positions
could not both be left as they were. **Five tags recast; no string in the run now stands more than twice.** The
device, its position, its two facts and every line of his dialogue are untouched.

1. **The Yuan tag.** *He said one thing that night and it was present tense and it bought nothing* stood **five
   times byte-identical — ch 253, 261, 262, 264 and 267** — with **one further variant at ch 255** (*in the
   present tense*), **one at ch 258** (*one sentence… and advised nothing*), and **four in the *evening* form at
   ch 259, 263, 265 and 266**, so eleven of Batch 0002's fifteen chapters. It is **zero in ch 268–298**.
   **Ruled on and REPAIRED at the close review** — five recasts at 261, 262, 264, 265 and 266; see the ruling
   above and the repairs table. *(Corrected at the close review: the close's own finding said the verbatim string
   stood **four** times and named only 261, 262, 264 and 267. It is five — **ch 253 is byte-identical** and was
   named in the same finding as part of the run. The earlier record also called the duplicated-paragraph repairs
   *five paragraphs across three chapters*; it is **four** — 248 ×1, 275 ×2, 295 ×1.)*
1. **The present-tense block at ch 297 (*What It Is*).** Four paragraphs of close third on Ning Yan in the present tense, in a book that admits one present frame and gives it to Yuan. **Recorded, not repaired.** The construction it uses — *It is a line in a book…* / *That is the whole of what she has done…* — is the volume's and the book's established register for a durable state and has direct precedent in the same volume (ch 239:31, 241:11, 249:23, 251:101, 252:49). No completed event is narrated in the present; the only present-perfect is *what she has done*, which is the flatness the paragraph is for. The rule's purpose — that the present marks his frame — is not in play, because the block is a register, not a viewpoint.
2. **Wei Ran is on the page four times (ch 243, 253, 269, 290), where `outline/volume-05.md` names Batches 0001 and 0004 in two places.** **Recorded, not repaired.** Every appearance is a rate, a road or an opinion in her own register; the operative hard rule — *no harem, nothing in Volume 5 is a romance* — is intact, and ch 290 was rewritten at this close so that the sentence saying so no longer says *nothing in this book*. Cutting two working cards to satisfy a batch-range statement would be a structural change and not a repair.
3. **Bai Zhu is named aloud in the volume only at ch 297**, where Volume 4's close recorded him as *named once at ch 150, spoken twice more at ch 189 and 196*. **Recorded.** The operative prohibition — no document he signs carries his name — holds, and at ch 297 he writes nothing at all. The one card in this volume (297) needed him named because it is the only place in Volume 5 he is on the page in person.
4. **`reconciled` is read on the page seven times across the volume** (250, 279, 282, 292, 294, 296, 298) where required fact 10 says *read once at the close for what it is*. **Recorded, not repaired.** It is read **once as the subject of a chapter** — ch 282, *The Reconciled Page* — and the other six are references in the course of other business, every one of them recording that it stands and none of them correcting it. The rule's purpose is that it is never corrected, and it never is.
5. **The volume never carries the four weeks every foundation costs her as a stated fact.** The outline says Volume 5 *carries them in the body*; what is on the page is the cost of each stage and the unnameable tenth item of the list at ch 291, which is the carrying without the arithmetic. **Recorded, not repaired** — adding the four weeks would be naming the thing `outline/ending.md` §8 reserves for ch 1000.
6. **`about four seconds` still stands twice after repair**, at ch 290 and ch 293. **Recorded deliberately** so the next phase knows the figure was thinned from four to two and why, rather than finding it and finishing the job.
7. **The claim's fourth movement — ch 237, 260, 268 and 294–295 — is four movements, not three.** The audit prompt counts three (237 → 260 → 268 → 294–295 as *moved* and *answered*). **Recorded** so the next planner counts from the page: it was read aloud, moved by draft, authorised onto the far-side book, and answered. Nothing is wrong; the count in the prompt compresses the authorisation into the answer.
8. **`workspace/volume-05-close/` holds no `.done` marker**, as every other finished phase directory does, and `workspace/continuation/` never held one. **Not fixed here: `PHASE_SYSTEM.md` gives the marker to the controller alone.** Recorded for the controller or the operator, on the precedent of the Volume 4 close.

### The state-file corrections, at the close and at its review

- **`state/open-threads.md`** carried the ch 251 interval (*three months ago*) as an unapplied proposed repair from Batch 0002, and this close applied it. Its *Still open* block also carried *Wei Ran appears twice* and the impossible measure. All three corrected.
- **`state/book-metadata.md`** and the Volume line above carried *101,160* as the volume file set. It is **101,266** after the repairs (101,204 after the close, 101,160 before it). **Corrected again at the review**: the close refreshed the volume total but left the four per-batch totals at their pre-close values, so they summed to 101,160 while the volume figure above them read 101,204. **All five figures are now derived from the chapters** — see the per-batch table below.
- **`state/chapter-summaries.md`** did not record the volume close at all, and its *Volumes 5–16* block still said the next phase was the Volume 5 close. Corrected, and a **Volume 5 closed — audited** section added beneath it.
- **The repair count itself.** *Twelve repairs* was carried in five files and matched no measure — the table has ten rows. **Corrected at the review** to the derived vocabulary above, and the reopened-chapter list rebuilt from the commit: **nineteen chapters across four batches**, not eleven across three.
- **The verbatim count in finding 1.** Stated as four and demonstrated at length as four; it is five. Corrected.

### The fifteen preserved items, confirmed one at a time

1. **Golden Core 3, how it was licensed.** Ch 292, day 600, past the line, licensed by no county paper of any kind, paid for out of far-side wages at five coppers a day and materials at far-side prices, entered in a wage-book column that is hers, kept by people who are not a county. 1 at ch 258 day 512, 2 at ch 275 day 556, 3 at ch 292 day 600. **The relicensed licence still says Foundation the first stage on the front of it and she has still never touched it.**
2. **The right index finger.** Lost at the far end of a blind run at about the sixth hour on the twenty-first of a month, in Volume 4, told out loud to a crew. Four fingers' worth of grip through all of Volume 5. **Since ch 288 the right arm is open half the time instead of a third**, which closed the ramp and the heavy end at that yard for good. **Nothing in Volume 5 gives any of it back and nothing offers to** — no offer appears anywhere in the volume.
3. **The relicensing legs in order:** clearance → seal → countersignature, Volume 4, not inverted. **Untouched in Volume 5.** No new clearance, seal or countersignature is invented for Golden Core 1–3, and no chapter says one was required or not required.
4. **What the boundary is, in the terms Volume 4 earned, and whether the tenth part has been given.** A line of work, tended by hand, paid by the day; drift; the residue; the layer logs a thing leaving a place. Ninety parts true, twice said (ch 199, ch 236, both in Volume 4) and **not again in Volume 5**. **The tenth part is not given in Volume 5** and nothing in it proves the crews wrong.
5. **What crossing cost, in hours and in people,** as Volume 4 closed it, plus Volume 5's own: five months of far-side wage-work at five coppers, bread from a store, materials priced for two whole hands, twelve gaps in a true column, and three working days at the end of a season nobody pays for.
6. **The claim: ANSWERED.** Eight hundred jin, the seventh and last condition, read aloud on the far side at ch 237, taken onto the far-side office's own book at ch 268, answered in ink at ch 294, drafted west, date in Hou Sanliang's counting room torn off by the hand that wrote it. **Hou paid, patient to the end, never met.**
7. **The referral and the empty space.** The space holds a date and *Held* (ch 210) and the countersignature on her petition in an office's hand dated from the far side (ch 238). **It is not an answer to the request. The request is still pending at the last page.** A Wall writ is not issued to a field agent. **The line's own people still do not know she exists.**
8. **Feng's Lung, and what the second will cost him — Volume 11, not here.** The first spent (ch 143); the second and any second appointment unspent and unmentioned. What Volume 5 took instead: his stair, his hand, his quarter — small and off page, through the district clerk's arms. What Volume 5 gave a home: **his fair hand copying the Foundation column, copies past the line into a book the far side keeps (ch 262), taken by a keeper's paste without knowing what it was.** Alive, warm, column at five, not thanked, never on the page after that.
9. ***Kiln-born* and the unreadable office.** ***Kiln-born* is still in two heads and on no paper — zero across Volume 5.** The Standing Office of the Second Account was read at ch 236, a middle office with others above it. **Everything above that one office is still unread and stays unread.** No Scribe, no Anchor, nothing about what he was before he died, nothing about her having seen him as a readout.
10. **The register still reads *reconciled*.** Read as the subject of a chapter at ch 282 and never corrected anywhere in the volume.
11. **The plate still worn: STILL WORN**, against her ribs, above eighteen counterfoils in four marks and the dead weight tickets. Described once, at ch 298, as an object she surrendered to nobody, described to nobody and would take off for nobody. **It is the object whose texture she remembers in ch 1000 and Volume 16 owns that.**
12. **Ledger: entered once without her order at ch 272**, at a price stated before the line went in, **and then closed as a standing held stage.** Hers to order no longer; **what replaces it is his readability, and it is said on the page in his own terms** — *I am entered* (272) · *a line on a board… does not need her to look at it* (272) · *I am kept, and I am keeping* (281).
13. **The year of non-forgiveness: STILL RUNNING, and not retired anywhere.** Touched once, at ch 297, at low volume, and it carries: *She did not thank him… and she did not tell him to stop.* She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.
14. **The channel.** About six weeks at the open of Volume 5, restored twice by listed jobs cleared and not by killings (ch 256, ch 290), each share intercepted, told before spent and not coming back. **About weeks at the close, and no figure of it stated anywhere in ch 239–298.**
15. **Wei Ran.** On the page as a line at ch 243, 253, 269 and 290 — a trade, a team, a rate and an opinion, and nothing else. **Nothing in Volume 5 is a romance.**

### The protagonist, answered in writing

**`outline/series.md` §2 names Yuan, male, as the protagonist and `state/book-metadata.md` records
Gender: Male. Volume 5 delivered the correction Volume 4's close promised and it is on the page. The
question this close still has to answer is what the protagonist actually did, and what it cost.**

**What he did across Volume 5, chapter by chapter, and what each of it cost.**

- **ch 256.** He took a share of a settled listed tending and **told her the price before he moved it**, and the price was two of the eleven holds in her left hand. *The share went the way shares go, intercepted and spent, and it did not come back.*
- **ch 258.** He stood in her own order — *You are ordering. And I am here* — and the layer said the one thing it had said since ch 237, and the turn went out of her bar stroke.
- **ch 272.** **He let a keeper's hand enter him as a readable line, away from her hearing, so that her hours would hold past the line without her.** The price was on the table before the line went in: **his unentered state, and the date of the morning he died.** *He keeps the apology. He loses the day.* **This is the first decision in the book that came out of him and not out of her account**, and the chapter says so.
- **ch 281.** He is on the page as a line under a lump of paste, and she keeps four hundred yards from the post on purpose.
- **ch 290.** He took a second share, on the season's last listed run closed, held it six days, **named the price before he moved it** — one line in the record the offices read, and the price out of her foundation, and *not one person alive has ever been able to tell you how much* — and she did not say no.
- **ch 296.** **He spent it, and the family account closed, and it cost her something she cannot name.**

**Every one of those is a decision by the book's protagonist. Four of the six were paid for out of her body
and the last two out of her in ways that leave nothing to point at.**

**What is now true of him that was not true at ch 238.** **He is readable from outside the bound without her,
and therefore findable** — and the chapter that made him findable said on the page, in his own frame, what
that means: *Being read means being findable… an office that looks at a line sends for the line, and being
sent for is a thing that happens to men in rooms with doors.* He is entered in the layer's own reading, on a
strip of cloth kept by people who are not a county, by a man who does not know he has done it. **`outline/ending.md`
§1 requires exactly this before the final volume opens — the Court stage, the Scribe finding him, and the fall.
The prerequisite exists on the page, in his own words, and a man the reader has seen in a room at ch 272 can
carry a fall.** **What Volume 6's planning may not assume: nothing more than this.** He is not stronger, not
restored, not located, not protected, and not forgiven. He has lost a day he will not get back. He has no
hands, no road, and no way of ever being anywhere she is not — he says so at ch 290. **The Ledger is no longer
hers to order and there is nothing she can order instead.**

**What may not happen.** This close did not re-frame ch 239–298, did not re-title the volume, introduced no new
final enemy, and took nothing the ending owns — **the bargain, the fall, Feng's death, the Anchor, the
Sisterhood and the Scribe are untouched and belong to Volumes 11–16, the Scribe to Volume 15.** The year of
non-forgiveness was not retired, he was not forgiven, and she was not allowed to tell him to stop. `outline/series.md`
and `outline/ending.md` are byte-unchanged by this phase, and `outline/volume-06.md` does not exist.

### The Volume 6 hand-off, restated as a fact on the page and not as a plan

> She finished the books of the bound from the far side of a line — the claim answered where it could be
> answered, the family account settled, the brother's decade put where somebody has to look at it, three
> stages taken that no county heard — and the far side paid every one of them into books it keeps by hand.
> But past the last working point the country goes on, farmed and lit, with offices above the one office she
> knows the name of that have never needed anything from inside a bound until now — and one of them has just
> been handed a column that is hers, in a hand it can read, with a furnace behind it. The question is not
> what the bound was. **It is what the country past the line does with a person it can now read, who owes it
> hours, carries a furnace it did not build, and cannot go back inside where she was ever announced.**

**And alongside it, as facts at ch 298:** Golden Core 3, licensed by nothing and paid for out of far-side
wages · four fingers' worth of grip and an arm open half the time, and nothing offers · **a season over, a
column closed on the thirtieth in another man's hand, eighteen counterfoils in four marks and no nineteenth,
and three working days nobody will ever pay** · **entered as good in a book she cannot be taken out of** ·
**the claim answered and Hou Sanliang paid and never met** · **Yuan readable without her and the Ledger no
longer hers to order** · MEMBER ABSENT standing three times · the register still reading *reconciled* · the
Clay Plate still worn · Shen Xiu unforgiven and unthanked · the three sponsors silent · **the year still
running** · **the Hunter's request still pending and the line's own people still not knowing she exists** ·
**and a mill two miles east whose wheel has not turned since before she came, which she stood half a mile short
of and did not go past.**

live next work is recorded in the cold-reader block at the top of this file and in `state/open-threads.md`.)*

to **write `outline/volume-06.md`** — *The Real Realm*, ch 299–360, goal fixed by `outline/series.md` §9: *In
this volume, Ning Yan must survive what is on the other side, or the crossing was a death and not an escape* —
and to open Volume 6's planning. **`outline/volume-06.md` must not exist until that phase writes it, and this
close did not create a Volume 6 batch directory.**

## Who is where at end of chapter 238 — VOLUME 4 CLOSED AND AUDITED (cold reader starts here)

Full record: `state/continuity.md` under ***Established facts, Volume 4 complete (ch 179–238)*** and ***Volume 4 running ledger — at end of ch 238***. **Day 470 — the seventh of a month.** She is **on the far side of a line of work**, in a country where nothing is announced, at **Foundation 9**, with a column of hours that is hers in a wage-book on a trestle, and about four fingers on one hand.

- **Ning Yan** — **FOUNDATION 9, reached at her own order on day 462, the twenty-ninth of a month, announced in the open at the far end of the working ground, and the first announcement in four years that was heard past the line.** **There is nothing above nine anywhere in the world.** Ceiling on the front of the relicensed licence is still **Foundation the first stage** and **she has still never touched it**; seven conditions referenced and never re-listed; the claim the seventh and last thing on the paper. **The stage column in four county books reads one, two, three, five, six, seven, eight, nine** — one hole in nine lines, and the hole is hers and she put it there at ch 234.
- **Body at the last page.** Short breath **four counts everywhere — three is gone and does not come back.** **The order is gone out of her left hand**, which has the heel gone with it, and every grip is built from nothing, one at a time, before a bar lands. **Eleven holds that hold to the end of a stroke, out of about seventy tried since the fourth of a month four months back.** **THE RIGHT INDEX FINGER IS GONE, PERMANENTLY** — lost at the far end of the blind run at about the sixth hour on the twenty-first of a month, **the finger that makes the turn at the end of a stroke and the finger a licence nail goes in with**, and **four fingers' worth of grip left on that hand.** **Nothing in the book gives it back and nothing offers to.** Right arm open a third of the time. Aperture cracked through and permanent. Not hungry since the first Foundation. Cold the way a stone is cold. Bread from a store at five coppers a day.
- **What the last eight chapters did.** **The price was stated before the word and the word was said** (231, 233). **She squared two true books and gave up making a run out of them** (232). **She hired onto the stretch nobody is hired on, at a trestle, in daylight, with her name said out loud for the third time on that ground, and nobody persuaded** (233). **She suppressed Foundation 8 at her own order, and for the first time the absence landed on a board the far side keeps** (234). **She cleared fourteen days of the blind run by hand, the trial settled, the channel came back out of her foundation, and the finger went** (235). **The honest partial answer was said once against a board and he read the office name above this county in one breath and stopped** (236). **Foundation 9 went past the line, the far side wrote it down, and a claim of eight hundred jin was read out loud in a room that can act on it** (237). **She crossed, was certified, and wrote her name in a book on the far side** (238).
- **The two ways the boundary came down, both on the page and in this order.** **First the layer logged it:** a dated line went into a tally-board that was already on the far side before she got there, in a column that is hers, and **he could see the line going in and could not see any part of her on either side of it.** **Then her body:** three-quarters of a mile of the bad flat, a foot and a bit on the low side all the way, quiet ground that tells you nothing, four counts of breath, **four fingers on one hand and a bar in the other**, a day and a half, and one fall at about a third of the way along. **Nobody found her and nobody was sent. There is not going to be a third.**
- **The far side is ordinary country.** Settled, farmed, lit, **a trestle with a book on it, a fire under it, eleven men on it, and a building with a stove in it and six men in it.** Nobody there has ever heard a stage announced. Nobody there asked her a question. **A wage-book on the far side has her hours in it, in order, with dates, and a man at it tore a counterfoil off the book and put it face down on the plank — six stubs in an oilcloth now, in three marks, and none of them checkable against anything.**
- **The far-side board on a post.** Nine years of a narrow column nobody on this course fills, filled by a keeper off a cart from the far end of the reach, in a hand nobody within nine days of road can read. **At the last page it holds nine lines. Eight of them have something on them and one of them has nothing on it at all** — and **she has seen a column like that before and nobody else in that country ever has.**
- **What went into the space on the shelf (ch 238).** **The countersignature on her petition, in an office's hand and not a person's, dated from the far side.** **It is not an answer to the Hunter's request and the request is still pending.** A Wall writ is not issued to a field agent. **The line's own people still do not know she exists.**
- **Bai Zhu.** **Named once in the book, at ch 150, to a form, and on no document he signs.** At ch 231 he attended seven days, wrote three lines, bought nothing, threatened nothing, touched her, entered nothing, asked nothing, and said all of that in one breath. At ch 238 he **certified the crossing in a Warden's hand, signed by the office and not by a person** — his second document in the book and the same shape as his first — and said the whole of the rest of it in one breath again. **He did not ask her to come.**
- **The office above this county.** **Read at ch 236, in one breath, for the first time in four years: the Standing Office of the Second Account.** A middle office, with others above it. **He stopped there and nothing after ch 236 goes one notch further.** No Scribe. No Anchor. Nothing about what he was before he died.
- **The claim.** **Eight hundred jin, standing, the seventh and last condition, travelling on the licence.** A date is entered against it in Hou Sanliang's counting room in Lowwater and has not been torn off. **At ch 237 somebody on the far side read the seventh condition out loud in a room, which put an eight hundred jin standing claim into the first office within nine days of road that can act on it in its whole life. Not resolved. Hou Sanliang unpaid, patient and never met.**
- **Yuan.** Voice. **Two spends on the column of marks in the last eight chapters — ch 231, which bought the price, and the count is four marks on the back of a dead weight ticket; ch 233 spent the standing, not a mark, at a price of one day out of three, held under a day and put down when the crossing was over.** **Merit settled once, at ch 235, on a finished job and not on a death, and he intercepted a share and spent it out of her foundation while the base was one stage short of finished, and it did not come back.** **The channel is restored and is about six weeks at the last page**, said once, in weeks. **Ledger is hers to order at the last page and she did not order it.** **He still has not told her what he was before he died and she still has not told him she saw him as a readout.**
- **The year of non-forgiveness was touched once in this batch, at ch 230, at low volume** — she goes on using him on purpose, **and it is not forgiveness and the account still carries.** She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.
- **MEMBER ABSENT stands three times and is permanent**, with the reason column a foot and a half wide and blank, and **the sheet went west for the last time this season.** No date west on any board after that man. The word was offered twice and no man has offered it a second time.
- **The course is closed against her and stays closed.** Two books in two hands, neither carrying a continuous run, **and three empty dates in the second one that are true and are never going to be filled.** Cui Bao said the whole of his position once and would do it again. **They are right and nothing in this volume proves them wrong.**
- **The register still reads *reconciled*. The Clay Plate still worn. Shen Xiu unforgiven and unthanked. The three sponsors silent. Ning Wu does not appear. What became of the eleven is not raised. The ch 150 refusal is not softened, deferred, reopened or regretted in any line.** Nothing about the sponsors' second book is lodged, copied or spoken anywhere in ch 119–238.
- **The crew at Qinghe is nine days west: a floor, nine men, and a tower with a light in it.** Said once, at ch 238, and not again.

### The four repairs, made in the chapters

**1 — ch 224, a named weekday.** *I would do the whole of it again on Friday* → *I would do the whole of it again on any working morning.* The rule is no weekday anywhere in ch 179–238; **the repair costs nothing, because nothing in the plot turns on the day of the week and *a working morning* is the idiom the volume already uses eight times.**

**2 — ch 232, a foreshadow of the finger, three chapters early.** *…and she had four fingers' worth of nothing on the other one that she had not noticed yet because nothing had been asked of that hand* → *…and every part of her that had gone wrong since the thaw had gone wrong in that hand.* The volume's rule is that nothing before ch 235 foreshadows the index finger. **The first form of this repair said *on that side*, which was wider than the body allows — the breath and the appetite both went, and neither is lateral. The close review scoped it to the hand.**

**3 — ch 179, the construction ch 235 needs, spent fifty-six chapters early.** *…because that is the finger that can still do a turn at the end of a stroke* → *…because a coin will go across the flat of that one and not across a fingertip.* The reason she used that finger is preserved; the phrase is not.

**4 — ch 220, the fifth stage on the wrong day of the month.** *…and it came on the nineteenth of this month* and *The stage came on the nineteenth* → *…and it came on the seventeenth of this month* and *The stage came at the fourth hour*, with *It came in the same week* → *It came earlier in that same day* and the two *two days* recast as *the rest of the day* and *the hours between the two of them*. **Day 360 is the seventeenth, the twenty-two days from the twenty-fifth of the month before land there, and the interval to day 386 is twenty-six only if the fifth is on the seventeenth. The chapter's second section heading is *The Seventeenth* and nine later chapters date the strike to the seventeenth, so the strike was never in doubt and the stage was the error.** The load-bearing argument of the chapter — that the two things are not connected — is preserved and in fact sharpens, because the two are now hours apart on one day rather than two days apart. **Corrected again at the close review: one sentence four paragraphs down still read *two things that happened in the same week*, and it now reads *two things that happened inside one day, hours apart*.**

**Nothing else in the volume was touched.** The planned plot, the sixty titles, the chapter order, the card beats, the four pinned stage days (386, 412, 440, 462), the pinned attendance day (389), both recorded deviations (ch 225 opening on the fifteenth, ch 228 running 412–414) and every ending stay where they were.

### Five findings recorded and not repaired, with the reason

1. **Bai Zhu's name is spoken twice more (ch 189, 196), against the outline's *named once*.** The operative prohibition — no document he signs carries his name — is intact, and both spoken uses are authorised by this volume's own batch cards. Repairing it would delete an authorised beat.
2. **The ch 234 cost is echoed at ch 236 and not at ch 235.** Ch 235 is the blind run, the trial, the finger and the telling. Putting the hole in it would fight every other thing the chapter does, and would make the cost a **third** naming rather than a second. Recorded, not repaired.
3. **Feng's fair hand copying the Foundation column, and his copies going past the line into a book the far side keeps, are not on the page** (row 16). He appears once, at ch 181, and Volume 4's own cards forbade a second appearance. Proposed repair carried unspent into Volume 5.
4. **No calendar month is named anywhere, but no weekday rule survives unwounded either** — repaired, not recorded, and listed here only so the record is complete: ch 224 was the sole instance and it is fixed.
5. **The far-side keeper gives her a knife and a spare strip at ch 236 *because she is holding her right hand in a way that made the tool look like a thing to be done one-handed*.** This is read as an outside man's eye catching something, and the narration does not tell the reader what it caught. Left as it is. Recorded because it is the only sentence in the volume that could be read as pricing the finger twice.

| 12 | **Ledger: held and refused, or held and spent** | **PASS — and this is the second answer whose shape has changed** | **TAKEN TWICE IN VOLUME 4 AND REFUSED ONCE OUT LOUD.** Batch 0001: **eight days out of about ten for a name**, price said before the word, at ch 187. Batch 0001: **refused once, out loud, at ch 191**, at the moment it would have been easiest. Batch 0004: **one day out of three for a crossing**, the price stated at ch 231 and before the word, the word said at ch 233, **held under a day and put down when the crossing was over.** **It is hers to order at the last page and she does not order it** (ch 238) |
| 13 | **The year of non-forgiveness: resolved or still running** | **PASS — and this is the third answer whose shape has changed** | **STILL RUNNING, AND IT IS A PURCHASE AND NOT AN ABSOLUTION.** The sentence the last page actually used is ch 230's: *She is not asking whether it is fair… That is not forgiveness. It is a purchase, and the account is still carrying, and it will be carrying at the end of the book.* **She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop. Do not retire it.** `outline/ending.md` is fixed that she forgives him in ch 1000 and it is housekeeping that closes because he is gone |
| 14 | **The channel: about how much at the last page, in weeks, and what it cost to get it** | **PASS** | **ABOUT SIX WEEKS**, said once, in weeks, at ch 236 and carried to the last page. **Restored by a listed trial cleared and not by a killing — fourteen days of the blind run by hand, Merit settling on a finished job at ch 235 — and paid out of a base that was one stage short of finished when he spent it.** Four fingers' worth of grip left on one hand, and it did not come back |
| 15 | **Wei Ran: whether she is still on the page at ch 238, and what she has** | **PASS** | **Off page, and once more than that.** Named at ch 221, appeared at ch 222, and **appeared once in Batch 0004 at ch 229**, where she offered **twenty coppers to be set down past the cart turn and picked up on the way back — a rate and a road, and not a favour** — and said the true thing about the bad flat: she has taken two loads that way in four years and went round both times, and about half the people who look at ground nobody hires on go. **She has a trade, a team, a rate and an opinion, and nothing else. Nothing in Volume 4 is a romance** |

### The protagonist, answered in writing

**`outline/series.md` §2 names Yuan, male, as the protagonist and `state/book-metadata.md` records
Gender: Male, so the male-led requirement holds at series level. That was never the question. The question
is what Volume 4 did with him inside his own book, and here is the answer, in the only terms the book allows.**

**What he actually did across ch 179–238.**

He **found a hand that wrote a date against a claim.** Eight days out of ten, price said before the word, on a
mat with nine men asleep: Luo Ping, thirty-nine, a schedule-man, sixteen years, with a wife and a boy of
eleven living past the county's boundary, and she had to carry that and could not use it.

He **held a standing for a crossing and was paid for it out of a base that was not finished.** One day out of
three, stated at ch 231 before the word, taken at ch 233, held under a day, put down when the crossing was
over, and spent out of a foundation one stage short of done.

He **intercepted a share of a settled job and spent it.** The job was the blind run; the trial cleared; the
Merit came on a finished thing and not on a dead man; he took a share of it and spent it and it did not come
back, and it cost her the right index finger.

He **restored his own life.** About six weeks at the last page, in the only unit the book uses for it.

And he **read one name he had said four times he could not read, in one breath, and stopped.**

**Every one of those is a decision by the book's protagonist. Every one of them was paid for by the woman, in
hours, in a stage, or in a finger.**

**Is that enough? No — and here is the finding, stated plainly rather than softened.**

**Sixty chapters of close third on a woman who is not the protagonist, in which the protagonist is a voice,
off-panel, rationed to four marks and then to one, is a structural debt, not a stylistic one.** The male-led
requirement is met at series level and will be met at every volume level, because Yuan is the man the whole
book is about — the layer, the cost, the thing that pays, the thing that is rationed. But **Volume 4 is the
second consecutive volume in which the man has no scene, no body, no room and no other character to speak to,
and the debt is now two volumes of sixteen old.** The batch that wrote it knew: the open question was carried
in `state/open-threads.md` from the Batch 0004 planning review onward and **nothing in the drafting of any of
the four batches settled it.**

**What Volume 5 does about it, decided here, before Volume 5 opens.**

The options the series outline permits are all still open. **Volume 5 takes the third: *the shape of Volume 5
is itself the correction*, and it takes it inside Volume 5's own sixty chapters rather than by re-framing
Volume 4.**

Stated as a requirement the Volume 5 planner must meet:

- **Volume 5 must put Yuan on the page in his own right for at least one sustained scene** — not more rationed
  sentences, not a description of him, not a dream. **A scene where he acts, or is acted on, away from her
  hearing.** He has never been in a room she was not in; there is now a country on the far side of the line
  where a man who keeps a board asks no questions, and a claim that has landed in the first office able to
  act on it, and the machinery of the layer is the only thing in the world that has ever moved him.
- **The cost of that scene is not optional and is not hers to pay again.** He has already paid the finger and
  the foundation. Whatever Volume 5 puts him in, it takes something that is his and that the ending still
  needs — because `outline/ending.md` is fixed that he refuses the bargain and takes the fall and is gone
  before ch 1000, and **a man the reader has never seen in a room cannot carry a fall.**
- **What Volume 5 may not do:** re-frame ch 179–238, re-title the volume, introduce a new final enemy, or take
  anything Volume 5 owns — the family account, Ning Wu's decade, the four months erased in Chapter 3, the
  bargain and the fall. **The Scribe is fixed in `outline/ending.md` and belongs to Volume 15**, and this
  decision does not touch it.
- **What Volume 5 must carry unchanged:** close third on Ning Yan for every external scene; **every sentence
  Yuan speaks in the present tense, and no other character ever in a marked present frame**; the fifteen
  continuity facts above; and the standing that his Ledger is **hers to order** and she has not ordered it.

**What it costs:** the volume's first instinct will be to put him on the page as a resource again — more
sentences, a bigger ration, a longer channel. **That is not the correction.** The correction is a scene in
which he is a person with his own situation and is not being spent. **It costs Volume 5 the easiest shape it
had, and it is worth it, because the alternative is a book with no protagonist in it from ch 179 onward.**

### The Volume 5 hand-off — Volume 5 is *Mortal Ash*, ch 239–298

> **SUPERSEDED AS STANDING POSITION BY `## Who is where at end of chapter 252`, which is above it in this
> file. This block is kept because it is what Volume 5's planner opened on and because its escalation
> sequence and its *New question for Volume 6* are still binding. Batch 0001 (ch 239–252) is on the page;
> Batch 0002 (ch 253–267) is the next thing to draft.**

> **In this volume, Ning Yan must finish the world they came from, or leaving it costs everyone she left standing in it.**

**The question Volume 4 opens onto it, as a fact on the page and not as a plan:**

> **She stood in the stretch nobody is paid to tend, and an announcement came out of her and went past the line, and the people on the far side wrote it in their own board because that is what the board is for. The bound did not notice, and cannot: a county book has no column for a thing heard past its own boundary, and nine counties that heard nine stages in order have heard nothing else. But the far side keeps a wage-board for a line it maintains by hand, and it now holds a figure in it that no county in nine counties can answer for — and a woman standing on their side of it whose licence carries a claim their side can act on.**

**And alongside it, as standing fact and not as plan:**

- **She is at FOUNDATION NINE, the top of the second realm, and there is nothing above nine anywhere in the world.** The paper still says Foundation the first stage on the front of it, **and she has still never touched the relicensed licence.** The seventh condition is the claim and the last thing on the paper.
- **She has lost the right index finger, permanently, at the blind run, and the crew were told out loud.** Four fingers' worth of grip left on that hand. Eleven holds in the other one out of about seventy tried. **Nothing gives it back and nothing offers to.**
- **She is across.** A wage-book on the far side with a column of hours that is hers, in order, with dates, kept by people who are not a county and are not obliged to keep it. **Nobody on that line has asked her a question.**
- **The Hunter's request is still pending.** The space took a countersignature at ch 238 and **that is not an answer**, and **a Wall writ is not issued to a field agent**, and **the line's own people still do not know she exists.**
- **The claim has moved and is not resolved.** Eight hundred jin, read out loud in a room on the far side, in the first office within nine days of road that can act on it. **Hou Sanliang is unpaid, patient and never met.**
- **About six weeks of channel**, restored by a listed trial cleared rather than by a killing, **out of a base that was one stage short of finished when he spent it.** **Ledger is hers to order and she has not ordered it.**
- **MEMBER ABSENT stands three times and is permanent**, with a reason column a foot and a half wide and blank, and **the sheet went west for the last time this season.** **No date west on any board after that man.**
- **The register still reads *reconciled*. The Clay Plate is still worn. Shen Xiu is unforgiven and unthanked. The three sponsors are silent. Feng is alive, warm, column at five, and not thanked. Ning Wu does not appear. What became of the eleven is not raised. She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.**
- **And the unspent item this audit added: Feng's fair hand copying the Foundation column, and his copies going up a stair that now belongs to a district clerk and past the line into a book the far side keeps.** Volume 4's own cards forbade him a second appearance and the beat was never given. **It is available, and it belongs to the far side, which is where Volume 5 starts.**

### Close review repair — the Volume 4 close, audited after the fact

**A review ran against the finished close and reproduced its arithmetic: batches 28,607 / 31,900 / 28,581 /
31,484 = 120,572, Volume 3 at 135,014, and `((360 − 134) mod 30) + 1 = 17` with 386 − 360 = 26. Markdown, titles
and the bracketed-line budget came back clean, the scope of the close was clean, and the four pinned stage days,
the pinned attendance day, both recorded deviations and all sixty endings were confirmed untouched. Five
findings came out of it. Four were real and are repaired; the fifth is the controller's.**

1. **ch 220 kept the old timing in one sentence.** *They were two things that happened in the **same week***,
   four paragraphs below the sentences the close had just moved into one day. **Now *They were two things
   that happened inside one day, hours apart*.** Nothing else in the chapter carries the old span.
2. **ch 232's repair claimed more than the body allows.** *every part of her that had gone wrong since the thaw
   had gone wrong on that side* is contradicted by two losses that are not lateral — the breath down to four
   counts and the loss of appetite, both established at ch 214. **Now *…had gone wrong in that hand***, which
   is what the fact supports and still scopes the sentence away from the finger lost at ch 235.
3. **The weekday rule was inferred, unenforced outside one volume, and broken eight times.** It is now **canon
   in `bible/world.md`** — *Rules of the world that hold everywhere* and *What must never appear* — and also
   world rule 9 in `state/continuity.md`: **the world has no named weeks; narration may not name a weekday,
   from any register and from any mouth.** The eight standing instances in closed Volumes 1 and 2 were repaired
   at word level: **ch 40** · **ch 47** · **ch 104** · **ch 109** · **ch 111 (×2)** · **ch 114 (×2)**. **A grep of
   `chapters/` for all seven weekday names now returns zero across ch 1–238.** **Reopening closed chapters is
   not new to this book — Volume 3's Batch 0001 review reopened two Volume 2 chapters, ch 116 and ch 118, over a
   date that contradicted the channel's own figure. **This is the first reopening that exists to apply a canon
   rule across volumes**, because the rule had never been written down anywhere. **Volume 2's recorded file set
   is unchanged at 111,226** on its own raw-count method, because the repairs replaced as many words as they
   removed; Volume 4's moves from 120,571 to **120,572**.
4. **Two records were wrong about the text, and both are corrected in place.** `state/book-metadata.md`
   described the word-count method as excluding *`##` section headings* when the figures also exclude the `#`
   chapter title line, and it claimed ch 179 was *twenty-two words over* the 2,700 guide on a raw count; by the
   method the file itself states, ch 179 is **2,683** and ch 199 is 2,661, so nothing in Volume 4 is over the
   guide. **One reviewer claim was itself wrong and is not adopted: it said no heading called *The Seventeenth*
   existed in ch 220. The chapter title is *Struck Out* and the section heading *The Seventeenth* is at line 9;
   the audit's original citation was loose about which it was, and is now written precisely.**
5. **Operational, and deliberately not fixed here: `workspace/volume-04-close/` has no `.done`.** The close is
   finished, every other phase directory in the repository carries the marker, and `novels.yml` gates dispatch on
   it, so an audited close is still eligible and will re-run. **`PHASE_SYSTEM.md` says only the controller may
   mark a phase done, so this phase has not written it. This is for the controller or the operator.**

**Carried deliberately and flagged: the protagonist correction is wholly deferred into Volume 5.** Yuan is the
named protagonist, has been a voice only and off-panel for two volumes of sixteen, and `outline/ending.md`
fixes that he takes the fall and is gone before ch 1000. The requirement is now a hard constraint on Volume
5's shape — **at least one sustained scene in which he acts, or is acted on, away from her hearing, and it
costs him something the ending still needs.** It is load-bearing on a phase that has not started. **Do not let
Volume 5 open without it.**

## What Volume 3 must not do (cold reader reads this second)

Written after batch 0009 closed Volume 2; re-checked after Batches 0001, 0002 and 0003, and again at the
**Volume 3 close audit**, whose full table is below this block. **Everything here is now history that Volume 4 must respect.**

**Do not reopen the Watch, the burial line, the county debt line or the no-surety request.** All four are
lodged, stamped and closed on the page.

**Do not put her back on the fifth hour at the intake table.** The line moved to the ninth at ch 95 and
stands there. **Do not audit the change.**

**Do not spend a second strike request or a second outside-bound office.** The slip at ch 110 is the only one
in this run, and the volumes on the high shelf are not copies and were not struck.

**Do not answer what became of the eleven.** Deliberately unexplained for the whole run; Volume 12 owns it.

**The naming is spent.** The stranger at the first desk is unnamed through ch 149, **named once, at ch 150**,
by himself, to a clerk taking the agent's name off a form, and **never on a document he signs** — including
the certification and the referral at ch 174, both of which carry the office and not a person.

**The licence is at its top.** Ceiling nine in her own hand, **nothing above it on the document**, seven
conditions never re-listed, the claim the seventh. **Nothing above Foundation is licensed without a second
relicensing, and that is petitioned, barred and not withdrawable (ch 175); only an office above this county
can complete it, and only past a boundary no county paper crosses.** Foundation 1 is Volume 4.

**Do not break the silence bought at ch 128.** Nothing about the sponsors' second book — the nine struck
households, the eleven-year spacing, the fourth line citing the maintaining members' ledger, the roundhouse
hand in the frame of twelve unsigned sheets — is lodged, copied or spoken anywhere in ch 119–178. **The seal
was bought with silence. Confirmed true across all four batches.**

**Do not re-run the suppression arithmetic.** It is spent three times: ch 104, ch 159–160 and ch 161. At the
reading, Sun He rules the three gaps with four words and stops. **A suppressed stage leaves the same width of
absence as any other hole; say it once and do not argue it again.**

**The audit and the Lung are spent.** Audit ch 136–140, offer ch 141, taking ch 143. He is a **Listener**,
alive, warm, coughing less, column at five, suspended from the stair for one quarter **at his own written
request** and **not dismissed**. **Do not offer him a second appointment or a second Lung before Volume 11.**

**Do not open a second accident, death, frostbite, starvation or rescue.** **Volume 3 killed one man, at
ch 140, and rescued nobody.** The lip at ch 165 was the batch's one piece of physical risk and nobody was
hurt.

**Do not record a chapter-length trade-off.** Guide 1,800–2,700; floor 1,300. **Volume 3 closed at 135,014
words over sixty chapters, ~2,250 average. Nothing is under the 1,300 floor.** Nine chapters sit above the
2,700 guide (ch 126, 127, 129, 130, 131, 132, 135, 140, 150 — longest **ch 140 at 3,193**) and two sit below the
1,800 guide (**ch 138 at 1,779 and ch 151 at 1,653**). Counts are body prose only, `##` headings removed.
**The Batch 0004 record's claim that every chapter was inside the guide was inaccurate and is corrected here
and in `state/continuity.md`. The guide is a guide; nothing in the volume is padded and nothing is truncated.**

**No bare Settlement fraction went on the page in Volume 3.** Channel was named twice in Batch 0003 (about
ten and the price at ch 149, leaving about five and a half; about three at ch 160 with the thirty-six days of
rationed use accounted for on the page) and **once in Batch 0004, at ch 178: about ten days.** Volume 4
speaks in weeks too.

**The crew is nine men and her. Ten sleepers in twelve bunks.** Never written as ten men.

**Do not let her forgive him.** Non-forgiveness runs from ch 100 through Volume 3 **into the opening of
Volume 4** — that is the year the milestone requires, and ch 178 does not resolve it.

### Where Volume 4 opens — all of it is on the page at ch 178

> **A Warden's office outside the bound now holds her name, her stages and her hours — in order —
> certified by her own county. The dismantling it has ordered can only be executed, and the Foundation
> relicensing she has petitioned can only be countersigned, past a boundary no county paper crosses.**

The rest of it, all read off the block above: **she is at Condensing 9 with a ceiling of nine and nothing
above nine on the document anywhere in the world** · the Foundation petition is **barred and not
withdrawable** on the ground that **this county keeps no schedule of work done past its boundary** ·
the district has answered the Hunter's request with **an empty space the width of a line** · **about
ten days of channel** against **about six weeks of winter still to come in it** · **Ledger is hers to
order and she has not ordered it** · **a date is entered against the eight hundred jin in a counting
room in Lowwater** · the season's road has been shut since day 117 and has not opened.

**The Wall has never been described.** In Volume 3 it existed only as *a writ this county does not
hold*, said once, in Bai Zhu's mouth at ch 174. **Nothing Volume 4 owns was spent in Volume 3.** The
outline is `outline/volume-04.md`; the first batch is planned at `workspace/volume-04-batch-0001/`.

## Volume 3 Batch 0004 — acceptance check, card by card

**ARCHIVED — NOT DELETED. Moved by the Volume 7 close review fix pass to `state/archive/current-closed-volumes-01-to-06.md`, under this same heading, and left this stub in its place so a search for the section still finds it here.** **This is the historical half of the run's position file: every block belonging to Volumes 1 to 6, including their close audits and close review repairs, and every superseded *Who is where at end of chapter* block.** It is off the hot file because the live position is the block at end of chapter 420 and every earlier block is superseded by it. Read it by heading when a Volume 8 beat needs to know exactly where something stood at an earlier close. Read that file for the section. Do not re-derive it, and do not treat this stub as the section.

## Volume 3 close audit — CLOSED

`outline/volume-03.md` has been walked line by line against ch 119–178. **Every named item is recorded below
with a chapter reference. All thirteen PASS.** The audit found **nine faults in the chapters and repaired all
nine**, word-level or single-number, in seven chapters; **six more are recorded as findings with a reason**;
and **four were faults in the state files themselves**, which are corrected here and in
`state/continuity.md`, `state/open-threads.md`, `state/chapter-summaries.md` and `state/book-metadata.md`. No
batch was replaced, no card beat moved, the single bracket stayed at ch 171, the irreversible step stayed at
ch 150, and every ending stayed where it was.

**Three of those nine repairs were later reversed by the close review** and the block *Close review repair*
below supersedes this one wherever the two disagree. **Counts after repair: Batch 0001 36,809 · Batch 0002
36,742 · Batch 0003 30,958 · Batch 0004 30,505 · Volume 3 file set 135,014.** The pre-audit figure was
135,020; the audit's repairs removed six words and the close review's three reverts and one age fix were
one-word swaps, so **135,014 is the figure both before and after the review.**

### Repairs made in the chapters during this audit

**Nine word- or number-level repairs, in seven chapters.** **No batch was replaced, no card beat moved, the
single bracket stayed at ch 171, the irreversible step stayed at ch 150, and every ending stayed where it
was.** Six of the nine were one figure, put at four where it should have been nine; two are Hou's counting
room being put nine counties away when it is **in Lowwater**, where she is standing; one is the winter's
length at ch 176. **Three of the six were the wrong half of the figure and were reverted at the close
review** — see *Close review repair* below. The audit's own statement of the rule is superseded there.

| # | Ch | Fault | Change |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 150 | **"about four hundred people in four counties who had heard a woman's body get to six and get to seven"** — the sentence opens *Nine counties* and then puts the announcement in four. **Correct: what is *heard* is nine counties** (V1 ch 46, *in four counties of nine*; ch 55, *going round nine counties*) | **nine counties.** Its echo at ch 161 repaired with it, so the two still echo exactly. **Stands at the close review.** |
| 2 | 153 | **"It had been dramatic in four counties."** The announcement, at four in the morning | **nine counties.** **Stands at the close review.** |

| 5 | 163 | **"A hole in a stage column in four counties"** — the audit changed this to nine on the authority of ch 132 line 217 | **REVERTED at the close review to four counties.** See *Close review repair* below |

Both figures land on the same end of the winter (day 184 + 11 weeks = day 215 + 7 weeks = day 219 + 6 weeks), so ch 176 was the wrong one | **about seven weeks.** **Stands at the close review.** |

**Six words left the manuscript and nothing else did.** The four-county figure survives where it belongs —
the claim at ch 132 ×2, 140, 148 ×2; the furnace schedule at ch 148 and 159; the Bureau's bundles at
ch 138; the sponsors' book at ch 128. **At the close review this paragraph and the repair rows above were
corrected: the footprint rule was the wrong way round, and three of the nine repairs were reverted.** Read
*Close review repair* below before using any figure from this audit.

**Verified after repair:** all sixty files have balanced speech; **zero** blockquotes, code fences, tables or
rules; **zero** weekdays; **zero** banned reflexes (*his eyes widened*, *he couldn't believe it*, *suddenly*,
*little did he know*); **zero** authorial scaffolding (*this chapter*, *this volume*, *the next chapter*, *in
this batch*, *the whole series*, *the reader*); four bracketed lines, one each at 135, 140, 148, 171; longest
paragraph 90 words; **Volume 3 file set 135,014 words** — Batch 0001 36,809 · Batch 0002 36,742 · Batch 0003
30,958 · Batch 0004 30,505. All sixty titles unique against 1–178 and none restates weather, hour, date or age.

### Findings recorded and NOT repaired, with the reason

1. **Du Hong's name is used four times in Volume 3** — ch 119 line 77 (*Du Hong paid me in a coat he stole*),
   ch 125 line 87, ch 138 line 89 (Feng's guilt over a false sheet), ch 160 line 19 (the suppression, *she
   worked the same grief with which she had worked Du Hong*). The outline's resolution list says **their
   names are not used again**; Cao Lei and Xu An are indeed never named in Volume 3, and Du Hong is used four
   times, all of them as remembered weight inside a live scene. **The burial line is not reopened**: no
   cause is re-argued, nothing is buried again, and no dead name goes into a document. **Not repaired, and
   the reason is recorded: removing the name damages four good beats to satisfy a line whose operative
   purpose — the burial line stays shut — is already satisfied.** Recorded here so it is not rediscovered as
   an unknown fault.
2. **"Eleven weeks" stands in fourteen chapters, twenty-eight uses, and carries at least five different
   senses** — the Hunter's span in this county (ch 150, 162, 163, 173, 174), weeks of the lane (152), weeks
   of savings (152), weeks of winter remaining (163), and a projection (151). **The state record was wrong
   on this.** The Batch 0004 fix pass traded it out of ch 153 and ch 161 (confirmed: **neither chapter carries
   it now**), reduced ch 173 from four uses to three, and left it deliberately at ch 150, 162, 163 and 174.
   The audit's sweep found it in **fourteen** chapters, not four. **The one instance that contradicted the
   final image — ch 176 — is repaired above.** The rest is **left standing by explicit instruction** and
   **carried out of the volume as a retired number**: *no new use of "eleven weeks" is permitted in Volume 4,
   and no elapsed figure may be built on it.* Where a Volume 4 chapter needs the Hunter's span, it is
   re-derived from **day 2 (his arrival, ch 119) to the day in question**.
3. **"About four seconds" is the volume's standard pause-length and appears thirty-six times across
   twenty-six chapters**, with a maximum of three in any one chapter (135, 148, 162) and never twice in a row.
   The Batch 0004 record claimed *one use at ch 171 plus the one-word beat*; that is **inaccurate** and is
   corrected below. **This is not a rule violation**: the four seconds and the release rule were established
   at ch 53–54 as his unit of speech, and none of these thirty-six is a re-explanation of that rule — they are
   ordinary elapsed time, varied by the situation, and the *release rule itself* is never re-explained.
   **Recorded, not repaired.** It is a rhythm, not a rule, and cutting thirty-six uses across a closed volume
   would be a batch-level intervention this phase is not authorised to make.
4. **Ch 177 carries no dialogue at all** — the only such chapter in the volume. Already adjudicated at the
   Batch 0004 review (item R4) and deliberately unchanged: one woman alone in a wash house with a licence,
   then alone with a hammer. **Confirmed here and not reopened.**
5. **Ch 166 ends on "She signed for her own shift and went to work."** Read against the paragraph above it —
   the cold end is the coldest place in the building, she is not posted there and it is not the crew's to
   post — this is the enactment of a decision, not a neutral action. **Passes.**

### Corrections to the state files this audit found to be inaccurate

- **"every chapter inside the guide and none under the floor"** (Batch 0004 record) — **wrong.** ch 138 is
  1,779 and **ch 151 is 1,653**, both below the 1,800 guide; nine chapters are above 2,700. Nothing is under
  the 1,300 floor. Corrected in `state/continuity.md`, `state/book-metadata.md` and this file.
- **"The *eleven weeks* figure now stands at ch 150, 162, 163 and 174 only"** — **wrong.** It stands in
  **fourteen chapters**. Corrected above and in `state/continuity.md`.
- **"The four seconds: one use at ch 171"** — **wrong.** Thirty-six uses across twenty-six chapters.
  Corrected in `state/continuity.md`.
- **Volume 3 file set** — 135,020 → **135,014** after the audit's repairs. Restated in
  `state/book-metadata.md`, `state/continuity.md`, `state/open-threads.md` and
  `workspace/volume-03-close/SUMMARY.md`.
- **"The name in the wrong order: five uses across four chapters (120 ×2, 129, 158, 162)"** — **wrong, and
  wrong in the close audit's own sweep.** It is **six uses across five chapters**: ch 120:15, ch 129:27,
  ch 129:29, **ch 149:5**, ch 158:15, ch 162:65. The second ch 120 hit is stones put back on a mat in the
  wrong order, not the name. Corrected in `state/continuity.md` and above.
- **"The footprint of the stage column is nine counties"** — **wrong, and reversed at the close review.**
  Nine is the heard footprint, four is the written one. See *Close review repair* below.

### Still standing, and not this phase's to open

**Nothing. The last item on this list is closed.** Ch 131 read *"She was twenty."* — `bible/characters.md`
fixes her at **eighteen in Volume 3** and ch 131 is in Volume 3. **Repaired at the close review to
*"She was eighteen."***, so the beat survives and the page stops contradicting the bible. No other chapter
in ch 119–178 states her age.

### The construction-budget sweep

**Spent and not reused as emphasis:** *there is no form* **zero** · *there is no column* **two**, once each at
ch 124 and 127, different meanings · *the record remained* **zero** · *nobody asked* **zero** ·
*nobody was thanked* **zero**; the one allowed instance is **"Nobody thanked her"** at ch 172 line 127, in the
past tense, where the girl has set not-being-thanked up as protection for two volumes · *nobody knew* **zero**
(the Batch 0004 record claimed one at ch 164; the actual line there is *he was not thanked*, and ch 164 line 57
reads *four of them done by men who did not know what she was* — **no instance of the construction exists**).
**Nobody said anything: nine uses across eight chapters** — 125, 131 ×2, 134, 154, 159, 162, 172, 174 — four of
them inside Batch 0003 and five inside Batch 0004, never twice in one chapter except ch 131. That is one use
per seven and a half chapters and none is load-bearing twice; **recorded as at the top of the acceptable
range, not as a rule breach.**
**The seven relicensing conditions re-listed: zero.** **The suppression arithmetic re-run: zero** — the three
gaps are ruled in four words at ch 174 and Sun He stops. **The claim-as-entered-act arithmetic: once**, at
ch 155, and not restated. **What a surety is for: zero.** **The signed-shift roster rule re-explained: zero.**
**The name in the wrong order: six uses across five chapters** (120, 129 ×2, 149, 158, 162), every one of them the
copyist's habit *used* and none of them *taught*; ch 129 is the one the outline permits. **The carriers' run
lists: ch 146 supplies them, ch 155 and 158 use them, ch 164 refers back** — a mechanism, not a refrain.
**The shoring over the cold end: zero** — she works it at ch 164–170 and it does not fail. ***The shape of a
happy life*: zero.** **Weekdays: zero.** **Authorial scaffolding: zero.** **`his eyes widened`,
`he couldn't believe it`, `suddenly`, `little did he know`: zero.** **No harem, nothing a romance, no new final
enemy.**

### Close review repair — this block supersedes the footprint rule above

The volume-close review re-audited this phase and **reversed the central rule the close audit wrote.** The
audit held that *nine counties* was the footprint of the stage column and the relicensed licence and that
*four counties* was the claim's and the furnace schedule's. **That is the wrong way round, and it breaks four
closed Volume 1 chapters.**

**The canon, from Volume 1, in the characters' own words:**

- **ch 46** — *the same advancement, **in four counties of nine**. Hanhui wrote that it was done for
  discipline. Shouyi wrote that no name had been given at all.*
- **ch 55** — *The announcement had been going round **nine counties** for three days, and Du Hong had read
  **her four counties' versions**.*
- **ch 53** — *somewhere in **four counties** four copyists were writing down four sentences about it.*
- **ch 55** — *There were **four copies of it in four counties**, because the announcement had gone out to four
  counties and each had written it down in its own book.*
- **ch 58** — *The design was on a document **in four counties**.*

**So: NINE counties is what is heard. FOUR counties is what is written.** Volume 2 says the same in one
sentence (ch 115): *An announcement is audible inside the bound.* Volume 3's own unedited text says it twice —
**ch 148:51** and **ch 159:61** both put the stage column and the annex on a furnace schedule **in four
counties**, in the same sentence as *nine counties* knowing about it.

**What the audit's authority actually was, and is not.** The audit cited ch 132:217 — *readable in order by
anybody in nine counties with a stamp and a reason*. That sentence is about **obtainability**: with a stamp
and a reason, a person in any county may get a certified copy and read it. **It is not a statement about
which books the column is entered in**, and it cannot license putting the column itself in nine books.

**Repaired here — three one-word reverts, ch 160, ch 163, ch 176.** All three are now back at **four
counties**: *a hole in a column four counties wide* (ch 160), *A hole in a stage column in four counties*
(ch 163), *a paper in four counties with three holes in it* (ch 176). **The three heard-footprint repairs
stand** — ch 150, ch 153 and ch 161 are about what people *heard*, which is nine. **Ch 178's *nine counties
that had it in order* stands** and is the reading, not the books; ch 171 (*It went out of the bound in every
voice in nine counties*) is the volume's idiom for it.

**This makes Volume 3 harsher, not softer, and that is correct.** One advancement entered in four counties is
entered four times and billed four times. That is what happened to her father at ch 55 and it is what the
three holes in her own column now do to her, in four county books, permanently.

**Rule as it now stands, for Volume 4:** nine for hearing, reading out, going round, or a certified copy
obtainable with grounds; four for anything written into a book. The rule is in `state/open-threads.md`.

**Also at this review:** ch 131's **"She was twenty."** repaired to **"She was eighteen."** against
`bible/characters.md` (*seventeen at the start of Volume 1; eighteen in Volume 3*). One word. See
*Closed at the close review* in `state/continuity.md`.

**And one operational finding, not repaired because it is not this phase's to touch:**
`workspace/volume-03-close/` had **no `.done` marker**, where `volume-03-batch-0003` and `-0004` both had
one. `.done` is written by the runner on the success path — every `novel: complete <phase>` commit in this
repository contains that one empty file and nothing else — and is a controller artifact.
**Recorded, not touched** — if a close phase re-dispatches, that is the runner's business, not a writing
phase's.

**Same finding, restated at the close-record review, and still not touched:**
`workspace/volume-03-close-record/` also carries no `.done`, and
`logs/volume-03-close-record.review.log` confirms the dispatcher's skip rule at
`.github/workflows/novels.yml` will treat that directory as pending, because its first prompt line is
`# Volume 3 close record` and matches neither the `outline` nor the `phase` token the skip rule looks for.
**No marker was created here.** The runner writes it when this review/fix pass returns successfully, and
a writing phase must not pre-empt it.

### The Volume 4 hand-off

The audit does not plan Volume 4. It records what is **true on the page at ch 178** and Volume 4 opens on it:

> **A Warden's office outside the bound now holds her name, her stages and her hours — in order — certified
> by her own county. The dismantling it has ordered can only be executed, and the Foundation relicensing she
> has petitioned can only be countersigned, past a boundary no county paper crosses.**

And alongside it, all of it on the page:

- **She is at Condensing 9, the top of the second realm, with a ceiling of nine and nothing above it on the
  document.** Sun He said so out loud in a room (ch 173) and a registrar put it in a county's book. The next
  stage is Foundation the first stage, and the paper that would carry her there is **petitioned, numbered,
  received, routed, barred and not withdrawable** (ch 175).
- **The district has already answered once**: received, numbered, referred upward, no instrument held in the
  district either, and an empty space the width of a line on a public shelf by a door (ch 176).
- **About ten days of channel**, said once, in Volume 3, in weeks, in the final image. **Ledger is hers to
  order and his to hold only on her word, and she has never ordered it.** **She does not forgive him and she
  does not tell him to stop.**
- **A date is entered against the eight hundred jin in a counting room she has never been in.** Hou Sanliang
  is unpaid and patient and has never been met.
- **Nothing Volume 4 owns was spent in Volume 3**: no description of the Wall, no crossing, no geography past
  a marker post, no Warden but his field agent's signature and the name of his office, which **Yuan still
  cannot read and has now said four times he cannot and is not going to say it a fifth time to be different
  about it** (ch 178).

## Chapter-length decision (settled once; do not re-record as an exception)

Batch 0007 averaged ~1,300 words and batch 0008 averaged ~811, against the 1,800–2,700 guide in `AGENTS.md`. Both batches carried the same note, "known trade-off, left intact rather than padded," three batches running. **That note is retired.**

Batch 0008 has been repaired to **~1,483 words/chapter, 22,269 words total** (was 12,176). The remedy was scene material, not padding: new scenes and beats inside the existing cards, never new plot. **Standing rule for the rest of the run:** the guide is 1,800–2,700; the floor is 1,300; **a batch that lands a monotone decline across its chapters, or an average under 1,300, is a failed batch.** Do not record a trade-off. Fix the chapters.

**Batch 0009 met the guide on its average without a repair: 28,234 words over fifteen chapters, average ~1,882** (was 28,236 before the volume-close word-level repairs, which cost 2 words net). Ten of the fifteen chapters are individually under 1,800 and are carried by ch 115 (2,657), 116 (2,083) and 118 (2,604); nothing is under the 1,300 floor and the run is not a monotone decline. The material added to reach the average was scene material inside the existing cards — the offering of four coins to the men on the wall, the clerk's apprentice at the sixth column, the good coat at the plank, Feng's three sentences stated in order, Feng writing for an hour on a stool she cannot watch from, the four struck copies and the shelf that was not struck, the no-surety request put in writing at the third hour, Lu Tao's board gone over in the spring, Shen Xiu's eye resting half a count longer on a signature than on a number, the coin put on a shut desk and picked up. **No new plot and no new card beat.** Every paragraph over ninety words in the batch was split before the batch was closed; the longest that remain are exactly ninety, at ch 109, ch 115 and ch 117.

## Proposed canon changes (recorded explicitly, not made silently)

**Canon change 1 — Ning Wu's register entry.**
`outline/volume-01.md` item 3 and its Concrete Resolution section say his record *remains* *assessed, reassigned, unreconciled*, not answered. The binding batch cards for ch 48 and ch 56 require her to buy the word *reconciled* with the sponsorship signature. The batch cards win. **Canon:** the register of Qinghe and of three other counties reads *reconciled*, entered with the hour, the clerk's name and the fact that it was asked for and not offered. The truth is unchanged: *assessed, reassigned, unreconciled*, reassigned above furnace authority at thirteen, alive, on the Warden's list. The proof of the reassignment is the Clay Plate she is still wearing. The lie stays on her head for Volumes 2–4. `outline/volume-01.md` was **not** edited for this at drafting time — controller-owned planning file — but it **was** edited during the batch-0005 review repair for two unrelated internal contradictions (Clay Tablet, Du Hong). If this canon change is ever reversed, edit the outline bullet with it.

**Canon change 2 — Qiu's line.**
*the shape of a happy life* is reserved by `bible/terminology.md` and `outline/ending.md` for a second speaking by Ning Wu in Volume 16. It had never been spoken on the page. It is now spoken **once**, by Qiu Zhulang, in ch 50. Used once. Do not use it again until Volume 16.

**Canon change 3 — the Winged Office.**
`state/open-threads.md` has carried "the Winged Office: the first thing in the book that reads a System" as a Vol 3 thread, but the phrase had never appeared in the manuscript. Volume 1 is now closed, so it is **not** named in the text. Ch 58 establishes instead: there is a place in the layer where announcements are read after they go out, the office attached to it has a name, and Yuan has never once been able to read it. Volume 2 or 3 may name the office.

**Canon change 4 — the Hall's roof.**
The Assembly Hall of Qinghe loses its roof in ch 52 and is never rebuilt. The open floor is used in Volume 5; the Hall still has no roof in Volume 6. **Confirmed on the page in batch 0006:** it is used for paper only, a clerk says so at ch 61, and water stands in its low corner from ch 61 onward.

**Canon change 5 — the Ashwood Nine-Step source.**
Confirmed still unshared. She refused to explain it on a floor in front of the whole sect (ch 51) and did not tell Yuan either.

**Canon changes 6–11 are recorded in the table above.**
